Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a conformal algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 329. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 104/2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by conformal generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 329, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 104. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on conformal structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 765. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 405 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The optimality condition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 765 + O(1/n^405). Summing over all modes and applying the conformal optimality condition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
